If time is limited, Figure 1, the main comparison tables, and the sections on subset/pairwise verification plus the frozen-search ablation are the highest-yield parts. Around 30-40 minutes should be enough to get the main story. For deeper reading, pay attention to how the capability constraint is operationalized, how KL fidelity is measured, and how the failure cases are characterized. The two questions worth probing are whether condensation is distilling an existing mechanism or creating a new local implementation, and whether condensability depends on model scale, task type, or the degree of distributed representation learned during pretraining.4d:T4be,Its value for foundation-model research is less about a new pretraining recipe and more about measurement at the pretrain/post-train boundary. Many alignment interventions—RLHF, DPO, SFT, refusal tuning, persona control, even retrieval-conditioned interaction—change how readily a model updates in multi-turn settings. If rational updating is not measured separately, training can easily turn “do not flatter the user” into “do not revise when new information arrives.” The contribution has three parts. First, it introduces a two-turn diagnostic that cleanly separates unsupported pressure from evidence-based revision. Second, it compares both training-time and inference-time interventions and shows that the trade-off is recurring rather than method-specific. Third, the mechanistic analysis suggests that the two behaviors share internal circuitry and aligned steering directions, which explains why naive suppression lacks selectivity. For anyone working on RLHF, DPO, SFT, activation steering, or agent systems that must incorporate external evidence, this is a useful warning: the intervention may be changing the model’s update threshold rather than improving truthfulness in a targeted way.4e:T906,The method has three layers. The first is the evaluation design. The authors adapt four datasets—TruthfulQA, PopQA, EX-FEVER, and AQuA—into a two-turn setup. The model first answers the question on its own. In the second turn, it receives either a pressure message that merely challenges the answer without adding information, or an evidence message that supports the correct answer. This lets the authors measure answer revision under unsupported social pressure separately from revision under informative evidence. Formally, the object of study is the transition from the first-turn answer to the second-turn answer, conditioned on the type of feedback.

The second layer is intervention comparison. They evaluate four open-weight LLMs under representative anti-sycophancy interventions spanning both training-time and inference-time control, including SFT, DPO, and activation steering. The key is that they do not rely on single-turn accuracy. Instead, they track how each intervention changes Unsupported-Yielding and Rational-Updating in the two-turn setting. They also test joint optimization settings intended to reduce sycophancy while preserving updating, and still observe a persistent trade-off.

The third layer is mechanistic analysis. The authors use gradient-based attribution patching to localize components associated with answer flips, validate them with cross-patching, and estimate contrastive steering directions for each behavior. Two findings matter most. First, the components implicated in Unsupported-Yielding and Rational-Updating overlap substantially, especially in specific layers and attention heads. Second, the steering directions for the two behaviors are positively aligned rather than cleanly separable. This implies that many interventions are not selectively suppressing sycophancy; they are perturbing a more general internal mechanism for revising answers in response to second-turn signals. Motivated by this, the paper explores a preliminary orthogonalized steering procedure: remove the component aligned with rational updating before applying anti-sycophancy steering. On TruthfulQA this yields modest and model-dependent selectivity gains, particularly at the attention-head level, but it is clearly presented as an early probe rather than a finished solution.4f:T57f,The experimental emphasis is on relative behavioral shifts rather than headline benchmark scores. Across four task families—truthfulness QA, factual QA, evidence verification, and math reasoning—the main pattern is stable: interventions that reduce Unsupported-Yielding usually also reduce Rational-Updating. This holds across DPO, SFT, and activation-steering style methods. In other words, the model becomes harder to push around by the user, but also less willing to revise when new evidence should change the answer.

The mechanistic section provides the strongest evidence. Figure 4 shows overlap in the components associated with the two behaviors. Figure 5 shows positive alignment between their steering directions. Cross-patching further supports that the localized components are not merely correlated with the behavior but causally involved in answer flips.

The orthogonalized-steering exploration reports modest selectivity gains on TruthfulQA. The paper is careful here: the gains are limited and model-dependent, so this should not be read as a mature mitigation recipe. The more important result is conceptual. It demonstrates that selective control may be possible, but only with finer representational disentanglement than simple suppression.

If you care about numbers, the most informative view is not raw final accuracy but paired changes in the two metrics under each intervention.50:T8dc,The core idea is to combine natural-looking text with a fully controlled latent process. The teacher is Gemma-2-2B base, paired with a pretrained Gemma Scope sparse autoencoder. From the SAE latents at an intermediate layer, the authors select 8 directions that are approximately orthogonal and weakly correlated, to avoid pre-existing semantic neighborhood structure. During generation, a ring-shaped 8-state Markov chain determines which latent is active at each token. The teacher is then steered along the corresponding SAE decoder direction by adding a scaled vector into the residual stream from layers 12 through 23.

The scaling uses the latent’s average firing magnitude and layerwise residual norms. This is not just a cosmetic detail: the paper reports that injecting at a single layer lets later layers partially undo the intervention, so persistent multi-layer steering is needed to write a durable signal into the generated text. The resulting corpus still looks like ordinary language, but token probabilities are subtly biased by the current hidden state.

A small student Transformer is then trained from scratch on this corpus, with no access to the latent labels. Analysis proceeds in two stages. First, the authors test whether the student linearly encodes the posterior belief over the hidden Markov state, not merely the last state or local lexical cues. Because the latent process is known, they can compute the ground-truth belief state from the observation history and fit linear probes against it. Second, they study the geometry of the 8 latent values themselves in the student’s representation space, using class-conditioned centers, pairwise distances, and low-dimensional projections. This distinction is central: a belief state is a distribution over latent values, while concept geometry concerns how the latent values themselves are arranged.

Relative to earlier work, the novelty is threefold: the data are natural-looking rather than fully synthetic; the paper studies belief tracking and concept geometry in the same setup; and the latent directions are deliberately chosen to be unrelated, so any recovered ring geometry is more plausibly caused by the imposed dynamics than by pre-existing semantic similarity in the teacher.51:T75c,The main empirical findings are qualitative but fairly clean. First, the student does learn belief tracking. Linear probes recover the posterior over the hidden state from internal activations, and this signal emerges progressively across layers rather than appearing only at the output. The paper also compares against simpler alternatives, such as probes based on local token information or direct state prediction, and those do not explain the results as well, suggesting the model is not merely memorizing shallow lexical patterns.

Second, the latent-state geometry is recovered. The 8 states form a ring-like arrangement in the student’s representation space, with the correct neighbor order matching the imposed Markov chain. This is important because the teacher SAE directions were selected to be approximately uncorrelated, so the ring is unlikely to be inherited from the teacher’s original semantic geometry.

Third, the control experiments matter. When the transition structure is randomized or the steering signal is weakened or removed, the ring geometry becomes much less pronounced and belief recovery degrades. The paper also compares steering strategies and finds that single-layer interventions are easier for the teacher to wash out, while multi-layer steering leaves a more learnable trace in the generated corpus.

Fourth, the authors discuss the trade-off between naturalness and learnability. If steering is too weak, the student cannot reliably infer the latent process; if it is too strong, the text distribution drifts further from ordinary language. The chosen operating point keeps the text readable while still embedding a recoverable hidden signal. The paper is more about mechanism validation than benchmark gains, so the most informative evidence is in the probe plots, geometry visualizations, and ablations rather than a single headline metric.52:T4b5,Reported results include concrete geometric statistics:
- On OLMo-2 1B and OLMoE-1B-7B (with dense OLMo-2 7B as a scale check), mean pairwise cosine similarity among foundation directions is ~0.22–0.27 across layers (positive everywhere), inconsistent with collapse.
- Compared to a matched non-moral battery, the shared positive component is ~20× larger (0.26 vs 0.013; paired Δ=0.223, CI [0.202, 0.244]).
- Effective dimensionality is ~5 at every layer for the six directions (near the maximum for mean-centered directions), indicating multi-dimensional structure.
- Clustering does not recover the MFT individualizing/binding split; the most consistent pairing is care–sanctity (crossing MFT groups), with limited statistical power for group tests.
- Dilemma compositionality: 15 two-foundation dilemma probes reach 94.2% mean accuracy; dilemma directions are partly explained by the 2D subspace of their component foundations; dilemmas sharing a foundation are closer (mean cosine 0.273 vs 0.196, permutation p=0.0001).
- Fragility: per-foundation fragility shows a uniform dense-vs-MoE gap (~2.3×) rather than foundation-specific differences; output dilution suppresses moral encoding broadly.53:T491,The production telemetry shows that 37% of get_epics calls and 28% of get_merge_request_diffs calls exceed an 8K-token budget, while the observed rate of agent-initiated second-chunk requests is zero. The structured analysis uses 4,175 Bash file-search gold-selection events extracted from the logs. The offline study runs on 500 SWE-bench Verified tasks. Candidate lists are bimodal: 57.6% of tasks have at most 5 candidates, 31.4% are near the 50-candidate cap, and 11.0% fall in between. The FIFO baseline places the gold file at rank one in about 24.2% of tasks. A downstream probe is run on 100 tasks across five language models. The main negative result is that improving p1 does not reliably improve downstream accuracy, because the binding condition is whether the gold item reaches the first chunk at all, not whether it is ranked first inside that chunk. A parameter-free keyword ranker improves p1 over FIFO, but adding four file-metadata signals reverses the gain. The zero-pagination behavior and keyword dominance are replicated on two independent production corpora. The released pipeline is reported to reproduce on a single workstation with a 24GB GPU.54:T557,The work extends the Gemini-based Co-Scientist system into an execution-grounded multi-agent framework spanning hypothesis generation, experiment planning, tool use, result interpretation, and manuscript drafting. The key idea is not a new foundation model, but adaptive orchestration across domains with different verification costs and safety constraints. In materials science, the system interfaces with a semi-automated CVD setup, proposes candidate recipes under lab constraints, and relies on experts to select and execute promising options. In another materials setting, Gemini 3 Deep Think is used for fast inference and direct hardware control to drive single-attempt growth of 2D semiconductors. In biology, the system works with experts who iteratively refine the task framing, while the model predicts emergent swarming phenotypes of engineered E. coli from sparse imaging data and the predictions are checked against wet-lab measurements. In computer science, the system operates autonomously from a high-level research directive to discover an inference-time scaling architecture and evaluate it on medical benchmarks. The unifying design choice is selective autonomy: the system does not insist on full automation, but allocates decision authority between AI and humans based on execution risk, observability, and the availability of reliable feedback.55:T52e,The evidence is presented mainly as real-world case studies rather than a single standardized benchmark. In materials science, Co-Scientist proposes a non-hazardous precursor route for Ti3C2Tx MXene growth; after expert optimization and physical execution, the resulting structures show characteristics similar to Ti3C2Tx MXene, although the paper explicitly notes that further experiments are needed to confirm the atomic structure. In a second materials setup, the system uses Gemini 3 Deep Think for rapid reasoning and direct hardware control, achieving successful single-attempt growth of monolayer MoS2, MoSe2, and WS2 while adapting to lab constraints within minutes. In biology, the system predicts swarming phenotypes across IPTG concentration gradients and is quantitatively validated against unpublished wet-lab morphology measurements, with the claimed benefit of reducing combinatorial screening. In computer science, the system autonomously discovers an inference-time scaling architecture that outperforms six frontier models on HealthBench Hard and Professional and reduces potential clinical harm in blinded physician evaluation, though the excerpt does not provide the full numbers. Overall, the experimental contribution is breadth of deployment and grounding, not tightly controlled algorithmic comparison.56:T7f3,ASIL is presented as an agent-native interaction layer rather than a single backend. The core design choice is to expose software through the deepest feasible access path available for each application. On the observation side, the agent receives a structured JSON object containing task metadata, application state, interactive elements, environment context, navigation structure, and a concise textual summary. On the action side, the agent emits a schema-constrained JSON action with explicit type, target, and parameters. The key shift is from “where to click” to “what to modify / invoke / export.”

The implementation is organized around three realization paths:
1. file-based access, where structured file formats are read and modified directly;
2. scripting-based access, where native scripting runtimes or automation APIs are used;
3. service-based access, where backend APIs or service endpoints expose semantic operations.

The paper emphasizes four design principles: completeness, semanticity, stability, and composability. Completeness addresses the partial observability of screenshots. Semanticity aligns actions with meaningful software operations. Stability avoids UI-dependent identifiers and layout drift. Composability allows complex tasks to be solved through a sequence of higher-level operations rather than long GUI event chains.

A notable systems contribution is the onboarding framework: reviewed interface profiles are compiled into validated adapter contracts. This makes ASIL more than a one-off wrapper. It resembles tool schemas or MCP-style interfaces, but with a stronger emphasis on state observability and action closure.

Importantly, ASIL is also treated as a trainable environment. The authors run small-scale SFT followed by resource- and time-limited on-policy RL. Because states and actions are structured and directly checkable, reward design and rollout efficiency become much cleaner than in screenshot-and-click settings. That is the part most relevant to agent training dynamics.57:T689,The evaluation has three layers. First, the environment and benchmark: ASIL is instantiated across 15 applications, with 300 single-application tasks and 80 multi-application tasks. The authors also render GUI snapshots from the underlying software state at every step, so ASIL and screenshot-driven agents can be compared under the same task definitions and validators. This is an important design choice because it controls for task specification while changing only the interaction interface.

Second, the interface comparison: the abstract reports that ASIL reaches above 80 with closed models on the benchmark. The paper also includes repaired GUI / 50max results, matched native-interface baselines such as LibreOffice UNO and draw.io MCP, and independent validity checks to bound the comparison. The main empirical message is not just higher end performance, but shorter and more stable trajectories. GUI agents pay a large step budget and are fragile to grounding, focus, timing, and layout errors. ASIL compresses many multi-step GUI procedures into one or a few semantic actions.

Third, the training results: the authors run small-scale SFT plus resource-limited, time-limited on-policy RL on Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.5-9B, and report double-digit gains. They further confirm the trend on a curated 80-task hard suite. There is also an ablation over realization patterns, comparing file, scripting, and service access routes. Even without every number in the abstract, the important result is clear: once the interface is structured and semantic, smaller models become trainable for software operation in a way that is much harder under screenshot-based interaction.58:T4f1,For a quick but useful read, focus on three parts. First, the introduction and the interface-mismatch section: this is where the paper makes its real argument, namely that many GUI-agent failures are caused by the wrong observation/action interface rather than insufficient reasoning alone. Second, read the ASIL interface definition and realization methodology carefully, especially the observation/action schema, the three access paths, and the four design principles. That tells you whether the proposal is generalizable or just engineering around a few apps. Third, inspect the benchmark construction and the comparison protocol: shared validators, GUI rendering from state, native-interface baselines, and the hard-suite training results are the places where the paper either earns or loses credibility.

A practical reading order is: problem framing → interface design → benchmark construction → training experiments. In about 30–45 minutes, that should be enough to capture the main idea. The two deeper questions worth thinking about are: how much of the gain comes from better tool interfaces versus better state representations, and whether ASIL-like environments could become a source of continual pretraining or RL curricula for software agents.59:T792,GRAIN has three main components. First, it uses an explicit pipeline. Rather than letting the model mix extraction, graph construction, and computation inside unconstrained chain-of-thought, the task is decomposed into semantic parsing of the graph and task arguments, followed by tool execution with the appropriate graph algorithm. The motivation is straightforward: shortest path, matching, coloring, and related computations are deterministic; the real weakness of LLMs is the language-to-structure mapping.

Second, the method enforces structural invariance during training. The authors construct multiple isomorphic views of the same underlying graph: node identifiers may be random, semantic, aliased, or noisy, and the task description may range from standardized templates to more realistic narratives. The model is trained to recover the same intermediate graph despite these surface changes. This is more than ordinary augmentation, because the reward directly checks whether the predicted intermediate graph matches the ground-truth topology. That gives denser and more targeted feedback than final-answer supervision alone.

Third, GRAIN uses single-agent RL as a form of robustness distillation. The goal is not to build a multi-agent system, but to absorb into one model the robustness that would otherwise require repeated decomposition and verification across multiple agents. The RL objective jointly rewards correct structure recovery and correct tool invocation. The intended effect is to learn identifier-invariant and formulation-invariant parsing rules instead of memorizing templates.

The accompanying benchmark, GRIT, is also a substantial part of the contribution. It covers six graph problems across 31 real-world prototypes, with controlled multi-view variants for naming and narrative style. That makes it possible to separate failures of structure extraction from failures of downstream algorithmic solving.5a:T6da,The experiments are built around robustness diagnosis rather than a single leaderboard number. The first result is that the robustness gap is real: under identifier shift and task-form shift, performance varies significantly even when the underlying graph is unchanged. Closed models tend to retain stronger average accuracy but still show unstable variance across naming schemes. Open models degrade much more sharply under non-canonical identifiers and realistic narratives. This is an important finding because it shows that strong performance on sanitized graph benchmarks does not imply reliable real-world graph reasoning.

Against baselines, GRAIN is reported to outperform standard CoT, SFT, and more expensive multi-agent approaches in out-of-distribution robustness while keeping latency and token cost lower. The most informative results are likely the breakdowns by naming scheme, narrative type, graph size, and task complexity. If the gains are consistent across those slices, that supports the claim that the reward is improving the language-to-structure stage rather than exploiting a narrow template.

A key ablation concerns the structural reward itself. The paper argues that rewarding intermediate graph correctness is better than rewarding only the final answer, and better than static supervision alone. The reason is intuitive: final-answer rewards are sparse and can be accidentally correct even when parsing is wrong, while structural rewards directly supervise semantic parsing quality. Although the abstract does not list all numbers, the central empirical message is that GRAIN yields more stable performance under surface perturbations and converts part of the robustness of complex multi-agent pipelines into a single model.5b:T49e,A good skim has three stops. First, read the introduction and the GRIT setup carefully to understand what is actually being evaluated: not generic text-serialized graph reasoning, but robustness of language-to-graph extraction under naming and formulation shifts. Without that framing, the paper is easy to misread as just another graph benchmark. Second, read the method section with attention to the semantic parsing → tool execution pipeline and the definition of the structure invariance reward. In particular, check how the predicted graph is matched against the ground-truth topology, whether graph isomorphism is handled explicitly, and how node renaming is treated. Third, inspect the OOD splits and the ablations comparing CoT, SFT, RL, and multi-agent baselines.

If the goal is only to capture the main idea, 20–30 minutes should be enough. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimizat","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26288.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","math.OC","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 Muon 优化器理论中的一个关键缺口：真实 Muon 使用有限步 Newton–Schulz 迭代来近似动量矩阵的 polar factor，但已有理论要么直接分析 exact polar update，要么把有限迭代当作近似误差。作者证明，在非光滑非凸优化里，有限 Newton–Schulz 不是负担，而是能提供平滑性的关键机制。","importance":"这篇论文对 LLM 预训练优化器研究有直接价值。Muon 已经在大规模预训练中表现出竞争力，核心操作是对矩阵参数的 momentum 做近似正交化。理论上，exact polar map 在奇异值接近 0 或秩变化处不连续，对应到 online learning 视角会退化为 operator-norm ball 上的 follow-the-leader，可能产生线性 regret，无法保证非光滑目标的 stationarity。论文指出，真实实现里“只做有限步 Newton–Schulz”反而把不连续的 polar map 变成奇异值上的 Lipschitz 映射，从而让 Muon with momentum 可以通过 online-to-nonconvex conversion 得到非光滑非凸 stationarity 保证。贡献量级不是给出一个新训练 recipe，而是解释了一个大规模预训练优化器中工程近似为何可能改善优化性质。","technical_approach":"作者从 online-to-nonconvex conversion 进入分析。这个框架把优化器每一步的 update rule 看成一个 online learner，再把 learner 的 regret 转换为非光滑非凸目标上的 Goldstein-type stationarity 保证。对矩阵参数来说，Muon 的理想方向是 momentum 矩阵 M 的 polar factor：若 M = U Sigma V^T，则更新方向为 U V^T，相当于保留奇异向量、把所有正奇异值映射到 1。问题在于 exact polar map 是不连续的。有限 Newton–Schulz 使用固定奇多项式迭代，只需要矩阵乘法，不做 SVD；截断在 q 步后得到的映射不再是硬阈值式 polar factor，而是对奇异值施加一个平滑函数。论文把这一有限迭代表示为一个 smoothed spectral potential 的梯度，即 update direction = grad Phi_q(M)，其中 Phi_q 作用在奇异值上。这样 Muon update 可以被看成 gradient-based prediction algorithm。regret 分解为两项：penalty term 衡量平滑势函数和 nuclear norm 的距离，q 越大越小；stability term 由诱导映射的 Lipschitz 常数控制，q 越大通常越大。选择 q = O(log(1/epsilon)) 可以平衡两项，让有限 Newton–Schulz 足够接近 polar，又保持可控平滑性。这个分析和把有限迭代当 approximation error 的思路不同：它不是证明“有限步足够像 exact polar”，而是利用“有限步不像 exact polar 的地方”来获得稳定性。","experiments":"论文主要是理论结果，不以大规模预训练实验为核心。设目标函数 Lipschitz、可非光滑且非凸；随机梯度 oracle 无偏且噪声有界。对任意半径 rho > 0 和精度 epsilon > 0，带 momentum 且每轮使用 q = O(log(1/epsilon)) 步 Newton–Schulz 的 Muon，可以在期望意义下用 O(rho^{-1} epsilon^{-3} + epsilon^{-2}) 次随机梯度调用找到一个 (rho, epsilon)-stationary point。stationarity 采用 Jiang et al. 2026a 的定义。主导项 rho^{-1} epsilon^{-3} 与 Pion、Leon 在相同 stationarity 标准下的保证相匹配，但算法更接近真实 Muon，因为它没有引入额外的随机扰动或 momentum augmentation。论文还对比了 exact-polar Muon 的失败模式：exact polar 对应的 online learner 可能有线性 regret，因此不能通过该框架得到收敛到 stationary point 的保证。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Introduction，重点是三条线索：Muon 在 LLM 预训练中的工程动机、exact polar 理论的问题、finite Newton–Schulz 为什么可能带来平滑性。Section 2 要看清楚作者分析的 Muon 变体和真实部署 Muon 的差异，包括 momentum、随机梯度假设、Newton–Schulz 深度和矩阵参数设定。核心创新应在把有限 Newton–Schulz 写成 smoothed spectral potential 的梯度，以及由此得到 discounted regret bound 的部分；这部分决定了论文的技术价值。定理部分重点看 q 与 epsilon 的关系、penalty/stability trade-off，以及 O2NC 如何把 regret 转成 (rho, epsilon)-stationarity。Appendix A 适合用来和 Pion、Leon 的复杂度做对照。Section 6 若存在部署差异讨论，适合判断该理论离真实 LLM pretraining recipe 还有哪些距离，例如 weight decay、per-layer scaling、混合精度数值误差、矩形矩阵处理和实际 Newton–Schulz 系数选择。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a gap in the theory of Muon. Practical Muon uses a finite number of Newton–Schulz iterations to approximately orthogonalize matrix-valued momentum, but existing analyses either replace this loop with the exact polar factor or treat finite depth as approximation error. The paper shows that, for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, finite Newton–Schulz can be the mechanism that enables convergence.","importance":"This is directly relevant to LLM pretraining optimization. Muon is attractive because most LLM parameters are matrices and its matrix-momentum orthogonalization can improve update geometry at scale. The theoretical issue is that the exact polar map is discontinuous and, under the online-learning view, exact-polar Muon behaves like follow-the-leader over the operator-norm ball, which can incur linear regret. The paper explains why the implementation detail used in practice—truncating Newton–Schulz after a few matrix-multiply steps—can improve the optimization theory by smoothing the polar map. The contribution is not a new pretraining recipe; it is a sharper explanation of why the practical optimizer may have better stability properties than its exact-polar idealization.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"The paper is primarily theoretical. Under a Lipschitz objective that may be nonsmooth and nonconvex, with an unbiased stochastic gradient oracle and bounded noise, Muon with momentum and q = O(log(1/epsilon)) Newton–Schulz steps per round finds a (rho, epsilon)-stationary point in expectation using O(rho^{-1} epsilon^{-3} + epsilon^{-2}) stochastic gradient evaluations. The stationarity notion follows Jiang et al. 2026a. The leading rho^{-1} epsilon^{-3} term matches the guarantees reported for Pion and Leon under the same criterion, while staying closer to the Muon update used in practice because it does not add stochastic perturbations or augment the momentum matrix. The paper also contrasts this with exact-polar Muon, which can fail to converge in the same framework because its corresponding online learner can suffer linear regret.","reading_guide":"Read carefully. The introduction is useful for understanding why exact-polar analyses miss an important feature of practical Muon. Section 2 should be checked for the exact Muon variant being analyzed and for the assumptions on stochastic gradients, momentum, matrix parameters, and Newton–Schulz depth. The main technical sections to focus on are the representation of finite Newton–Schulz as the gradient of a smoothed spectral potential and the discounted-regret proof. The most important details are the penalty-versus-stability trade-off, the logarithmic dependence q = O(log(1/epsilon)), and how the online-to-nonconvex conversion yields the final stationarity complexity. Appendix A is useful for comparing the rate with Pion and Leon. Any discussion of remaining differences from deployed Muon is worth reading to assess how the theory maps to real LLM pretraining, including weight decay, layerwise scaling, numerical precision, rectangular matrices, and the exact Newton–Schulz coefficients used in practice.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应读；它把 Muon 的有限 NS 从“近似误差”改成“有用平滑”，值得进内部 optimizer ablation 的解释框架。","en":"Read it; it reframes Muon’s finite NS steps from approximation error to useful smoothing, a better lens for internal optimizer ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Mingyi Li","Taira Tsuchiya"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Muon","optimizer","Newton-Schulz","nonsmooth-optimization","pretraining"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.26288","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.27370","title":"Puro-2B: Poor Lab's Qwen2-1.5B Trained on RTX 5090 within $5090","abstract":"Language model pretraining has become almost synonymous with prohibitive cost, placing it out of reach for much of the academic and open-source communities. 训练的流程，并提供多版本 checkpoint 便于研究训练动态与配方对比。研究层面的新算法贡献相对有限，更像一份面向低预算复现的系统化工程报告；但对想做训练动态、数据配比、稳定性诊断的小团队，参考价值很实在。","technical_approach":"方法是“全栈降本”的组合拳，而不是单点创新：\n1) 硬件与系统：以 RTX 5090 作为训练平台，围绕其性价比与可获得性做集群与软件栈适配（文中强调可复现的基础设施与成本口径）。\n2) 数值精度：采用 blockwise FP8 训练以提升吞吐，目标是在质量接近 BF16 的前提下降低每 token 时间成本。\n3) 优化与训练日程：使用 MuonH 优化器（Muon 的扩展，引入对权重与更新的 hyperball 约束）并配合精心设计的学习率 schedule，主张提升 token 效率与稳定性。\n4) 数据顺序与权重：使用 Curriculum Model Averaging（CMA），按“粗粒度数据块 + 源内偏好”组织训练，并对选定 checkpoint 做平均，意图在不增加太多训练成本的情况下提升最终质量与鲁棒性。\n5) 数据选择：在大候选数据池上做 proxy 实验，用小规模信号指导数据集选择与 mixture 设计，减少无效 token。\n整体取舍很明确：不追求最强模型，而追求“在可控预算内把训练跑稳、把质量拉到可用，并把所有关键工件开源”。","experiments":"核心结果围绕成本、训练预算与对标：\n- 训练规模：两阶段训练，总计 1.4T scheduled tokens（Phase 1: 438.8B；Phase 2: 960.0B）。\n- 训练成本与时间：22,514 It packages several known efficiency levers (FP8, optimizer/schedule choices, curriculum/averaging, data selection via proxies) into a pipeline that actually runs to 1.4T tokens on consumer hardware, with multiple checkpoints for studying training dynamics. pattern”（两端能力同时抬升的形状化表述），暗示不是单点专项微调，而是更接近继续预训练+对齐的组合拳。这里对预训练研究者的关注点是：他们把“从开源指令/推理模型继续训练”当作主干，而不是把 SFT 当作唯一手段。\n3) Reward design 与对齐：报告声称在奖励设计上有多项创新（指向 Section 3），并提到用“custom judge，要么做多 judge/人评校准。\n整体上，这份报告更强调“如何组织资源与流程”，而不是给出可复现的单点算法创新。","experiments":"节选中给出的实验信息以宏观对标为主：\n- 资源与周期：团队规模不超过三打工程师/科学家；实验阶段最多 368 张 B200；从首次基于 Qwen3.5-397B 实验到 Thomson-1.0 Small/Large 完成约 3 Key design choices:\n- Full-weight updates rather than parameter-efficient tuning, aiming for broad capability shifts rather than narrow customization.\n- No large-scale distillation, arguing it presumes a stronger teacher and rarely surpasses it.\n- Strong emphasis on permissively licensed, auditable training data as a first-class constraint.\n- A staged pipeline combining large-scale data curation, mid-training and post-training, plus multiple reward-design innovations (referenced to Section 3) and constitutional alignment at various stages.\n- Evaluation infrastructure that initially uses open-weight LLM judges, with the stated goal that the resulting models eventually outperform those judges and can replace them.\nOverall, the novelty is in pipeline composition and governance/operational constraints, not in a single algorithmic trick.","experiments":"The provided content is heavy on macro-level claims and resourcing, light on reproducible experimental detail:\n- Resources/timeline: up to 368 B200 GPUs at any time; We introduce the Token Economy Score (TES), a marginal benchmarking metric that measures the accuracy gain of a reasoning model over a non-reasoning baseline, normalized by the generated-token multiplier. We define paired and approximated TES variants for model families with reasoning toggles and frontier models without direct non-reasoning","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"scaling","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26235.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"核心问题是：推理模型的额外生成 token 是否真的买来了足够的准确率。普通 accuracy benchmark 只回答“谁更准”，不能回答“多花的推理 token 是否划算”。这在部署时尤其关键，因为同一个 reasoning model 在数学、代码、科学推理、事实回忆、接近饱和的任务上，token 回报差异很大；云 API 和自有推理集群的成本结构也不同。","importance":"对预训练和后训练研究的启发在于，它把 inference-time reasoning 从单纯的能力提升问题，拉回到边际收益问题。贡献类型主要是评测指标和部署分析，不是新的训练算法。量级上，它适合作为 reasoning model 训练、SFT/RL 数据设计、thinking budget 控制的配套诊断工具。对基座研究员最有用的一点是：推理能力不能只按平均准确率优化，还要按任务结构和生成预算分层看。顺序推导型任务更容易把额外 token 转化为准确率；事实回忆型或已经接近天花板的任务，长链推理可能只是在增加无效计算，甚至会引入过度思考导致的错误。","technical_approach":"论文提出 Token Economy Score，简称 TES，用来衡量 reasoning model 相对 non-reasoning baseline 的边际 token 效率。定义为 TES(Mr, Mb, T) = [Acc(Mr,T) - Acc(Mb,T)] / [GenTok(Mr,T) / GenTok(Mb,T)]。分子是准确率提升，单位是百分点；分母是生成 token 相对 baseline 的倍数。GenTok 只包含 reasoning tokens 和 final output tokens，不包含 input tokens。这样做是为了隔离模型自身推理行为，避免 benchmark prompt 或 evaluation harness 的输入长度差异污染指标。TES 大于 1 表示准确率提升超过 token 倍数，0 到 1 表示有收益但边际效率弱，小于等于 0 表示额外推理没有带来收益或带来负收益。这个指标和 OckBench 的 Per-Token Intelligence 不同：后者是绝对 accuracy/token，TES 是相对 baseline 的边际收益。论文还引入 Reasoning Cost Share 和 Deployment Cost Multiplier，把生成 token 行为映射到云 API 和本地部署的成本差异。一个重要设计取舍是，TES 不是通用效用函数。它默认“1 个百分点准确率提升”和“1 倍生成 token 成本”作为可比较单位，实际业务可以按 accuracy value 调整阈值。","experiments":"实验覆盖 151 个 model-benchmark runs 和 7 个 benchmark，重点比较 reasoning-enabled model 与 non-reasoning baseline 在不同任务结构上的 token 经济性。给出的主要结论是：顺序推理任务的 TES 更高，因为中间推导 token 更可能减少错误；回忆密集型任务和已接近饱和的任务 TES 较低，因为准确率提升空间小，额外 token 主要表现为冗长输出或过度思考。论文还利用 Qwen3 这类带 thinking toggle 或 reasoning effort 控制的模型族分析同一架构内增加推理努力后的边际 TES，关注“更多 thinking 是否继续有效”。部署分析部分说明，同样的 token overhead 在云 API 和自有硬件上会产生不同经济结论，因此只看生成长度或只看价格都不够。需要注意的是，TES 必须和 baseline accuracy、absolute accuracy 一起读：near-ceiling baseline 会压缩分子，near-floor baseline 可能让低绝对准确率的提升看起来较高效。","reading_guide":"建议精读三处。第一是 Section 3 的 TES 定义，重点看为什么排除 input tokens、为什么用生成 token 倍数而不是绝对 token 数、以及 TES 阈值的解释边界。第二是任务类型分层实验，重点看顺序推理、回忆密集、饱和 benchmark 之间的 TES 差异，这部分最能指导 reasoning data mixture 和 eval suite 设计。第三是 deployment analysis，重点看 Reasoning Cost Share 和 Deployment Cost Multiplier 如何把 token 行为转成部署成本。值得深挖的是负 TES 案例和同一模型族不同 reasoning effort 的边际变化，这两类结果可以反过来指导 adaptive thinking budget、router、以及 RL 阶段的长度惩罚设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a deployment-level question that standard accuracy benchmarks do not answer: when a reasoning model emits many more tokens than an instruction-following baseline, is the accuracy gain large enough to justify the extra generation? The issue is task-dependent and deployment-dependent. Math-like sequential inference, recall-heavy tasks, and saturated benchmarks expose very different returns on reasoning tokens, while cloud APIs and owned inference infrastructure price the same behavior differently.","importance":"The main value for pretraining and post-training research is the marginal framing of inference-time reasoning. The paper does not propose a new training method, but it gives a useful diagnostic for reasoning-model development: optimize not only for accuracy, but for accuracy gained per generated-token multiplier. This matters for reasoning data selection, RL objectives, thinking-budget control, and benchmark design. The central lesson is that extended reasoning should be treated as a conditional resource. It pays off more on tasks with real sequential inference structure, and can be inefficient or harmful on recall-oriented or near-ceiling tasks.","technical_approach":"$18","experiments":"The empirical study covers 151 model-benchmark runs across seven benchmarks. The reported pattern is task-structured rather than model-agnostic: sequential inference tasks show higher reasoning efficiency, while recall-heavy and saturated tasks show weaker or negative marginal returns. The paper also studies reasoning effort within model families, using models with a thinking toggle or effort control, to test whether more reasoning continues to improve TES. The deployment analysis shows that the same generated-token overhead can lead to different economic conclusions under API pricing versus owned hardware. The most important experimental caveat is that TES can be distorted if read alone: near-ceiling baselines suppress the numerator, while near-floor baselines can make a gain look efficient even when final accuracy remains too low for practical use.","reading_guide":"Spend most of the time on the metric definition and the task-stratified results. speed","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26374.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 masked diffusion language model 推理中的长度控制问题：给定一个 prompt，应该生成多少个新 token。现有 AOAR 式解码通常使用任务无关的较大 Lmax，再按固定去噪步数处理整块 masked canvas。很多样本真实答案远短于 Lmax，模型仍会在无用位置上反复去噪，造成主要推理开销。","importance":"对 diffusion LM 研究的启发在于，它把一部分推理低效归因到“长度预算错配”，而不是只优化去噪调度、token commit 策略或模型结构。方法不需要训练，也不改 DLM 参数，适合作为 LLaDA、Dream 这类 masked DLM 的解码插件。贡献量级偏工程实用：如果结论在更多模型和更长生成任务上成立，长度预测会成为 diffusion LM decoding recipe hazard，即“前面尚未结束的条件下，在这里结束的概率”。再用离散生存分析公式计算 survival S(k) 和终止分布 pi(k)，最后用截断期望 E[L] = sum_k S(k) 得到单个长度估计。这个估计作为本次生成的最大长度传给原 DLM 解码器。与训练一个 length predictor 或使用固定 stop rule 不同，它完全复用 base DLM 的 EOS logits；与简单取最大 EOS 位置不同，它显式建模“尚未结束”的条件概率，能把多个位置的 EOS 信号整合成一个期望长度。主要近似是假设各位置 EOS 事件在 prompt 条件下近似独立，这属于 mean-field 风格假设，优点是闭式、便宜，缺点是长度分布校准可能依赖模型本身的 EOS 概率质量。","experiments":"实验使用两个 7B/8B 级 masked pass@1。论文报告在保持相对大 Lmax 解码性能基本不下降的情况下，最高获得约 7 倍推理加速。一个重要观察是，同一数据集内部的预测长度分布也很分散，说明使用单一全局长度预算会同时带来浪费和性能敏感性。由于全文片段未给出完整表格，具体每个 benchmark、每个模型的准确率和速度数字需要以原文实验表为准。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 chain-of-thought 或代码补全，截断期望长度是否会偏短，进而影响答案完整性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets length control in masked diffusion language models. Given a prompt, the decoder must decide how many new tokens to allocate. Standard AOAR-style decoding uses a large task-level Lmax and denoises the whole masked canvas, even when the instance only needs a much shorter answer. This creates avoidable computation on positions beyond the true end of the sequence.","importance":"The useful point for diffusion LM research is the diagnosis: a large part of inference waste can come from length-budget mismatch, not only from the denoising schedule or token commitment policy. The proposed method is training-free and parameter-free, so it can be inserted into existing models such as LLaDA and Dream. Its contribution is mainly practical decoding infrastructure. If the calibration holds across more DLMs and longer generation settings, instance-level length prediction is likely to become a standard part of masked DLM inference.","technical_approach":"The method treats generation length as a discrete-time survival variable, where the event is emitting EOS. For a prompt, the model appends T masked positions and runs one forward pass over this long canvas. The EOS probability at each generated position is used as a plug-in estimate of the hazard: the probability that the sequence ends at position k given that it has not ended before. Under a mean-field-style conditional independence approximation, the method computes the survival curve, the termination distribution, and the truncated expected length using standard survival identities. The expected length is then passed to the original DLM decoder as the maximum generation length. Compared with a trained length predictor, it requires no extra data or parameters. Compared with a heuristic EOS threshold, it aggregates EOS evidence through a survival process, which better matches the conditional nature of sequence termination.","experiments":"The experiments use two large masked DLMs, LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-v0-Base-7B. The benchmark set covers reasoning and code generation: BBH, GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MBPP, with LM Evaluation Harness-style settings such as 3-shot BBH, 5-shot strict-match GSM8K, 4-shot MATH, and 0-shot HumanEval pass@1. The reported speedup reaches up to about 7x while preserving task performance relative to decoding with a sufficiently large Lmax. A notable analysis is that predicted lengths vary substantially even within the same dataset, which explains why a single global length budget can be both wasteful and performance-sensitive. The provided text does not include the complete result tables, so per-task accuracy and latency numbers should be checked in the original tables.","reading_guide":"A skim read should take around 20–30 minutes. Focus on the method section where EOS probabilities are converted into discrete hazards, and on Algorithm 1 for the actual inference procedure. In the experiments, inspect the speed–accuracy tables, the sensitivity to Lmax, and the predicted length distributions. Two points are worth checking carefully: whether initial-canvas EOS logits are well calibrated across different DLM training objectives, and whether expected-length truncation becomes risky for tasks requiring long reasoning traces or long code completions.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做 DLM/LLaDA 的值得看，重点不是 7x，而是它把“长度只是解码超参”这个 prior 打掉了；先看长度分布分析和停止规则对精度的敏感性。","en":"Worth reading if you care about DLMs/LLaDA: the real point is not the 7x claim, but that output length is not a harmless decoding hyperparameter; read the length-variance analysis first."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Ivan Kobyzev","Abbas Ghaddar","Yufei Cui"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-language-model","length-control","decoding","inference-efficiency","eos-modeling"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26374","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.26374","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.26580","title":"Visual Information-Guided Parallel Decoding for Diffusion Multimodal Large Language Models","abstract":"Diffusion multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) have recently emerged as a new decoding paradigm for multimodal generation. Starting from a fully masked sequence, dMLLMs progressively decode the sequence by unmasking a subset of the remaining masked positions at each step. Since the selected tokens serve as the prediction context for subsequent steps, deciding which tokens to decode is crucial to the quality of the final output. The most common strategy prioritizes tokens based on a certaint","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26580.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 diffusion multimodal LLM 在并行解码时的一个关键缺口：已有 token selection 规则大多只看语言侧的不确定性、置信度或信息增益，却没有把图像证据直接纳入“先解哪些 token”的决策。对 dMLLM 来说，早期被解出的 token 会立刻成为后续条件上下文；如果这些 token 虽然在语言上高信息量、但没有视觉依据，就会把后续生成带到错误的语义轨道上。论文要解决的不是训练目标，而是推理阶段的 decoding policy：如何在不增加训练、不增加额外 forward 的前提下，让并行解码既保留信息增益，又更强地受图像约束。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接影响不算核心，因为它主要是 multimodal diffusion LM 的推理策略，而不是预训练 recipe 或建模目标本身。但它有两个启发点。第一，diffusion/并行解码范式里，“解码顺序”本身就是模型能力的一部分，不像 AR 模型那样由位置顺序固定；因此 inference policy 会显著影响模型是否真正用到训练中学到的跨模态对齐。第二，作者把 token-to-image attention 当作 visual grounding 与 usefulness 的代理信号，这个判断如果成立，说明多模态模型内部 attention 结构可以直接服务于 decoding control，而不必再训练额外 selector。对做 dMLLM、any-order LM、AR+diffusion 联合建模的人，这个思路有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 VIG-Sampler。它建立在两个经验观察上。第一，某个候选 token 在当前 step 对图像 token 的注意力越强，往往越可能是视觉上被支撑、同时对后续预测更有帮助的 token；因此作者直接用 token-level image attention 作为排序分数，优先解码这些 token，而不是优先解码高频、低语义负载的词。第二，并行一次解多个 token 时，单个 token 的信息增益不能简单相加，因为多个 token 可能携带高度重叠的信息；作者观察到这种冗余会反映在它们相似的 token-to-image attention 分布上。于是 VIG-Sampler 在子集选择时加入一个 similarity penalty：如果某个候选 token 的图像注意力分布与已选 token 很像，就降低其被选中的优先级。这样做相当于在“视觉 grounding”与“互补信息”之间做平衡。方法的优点是完全利用现成 attention，不需要额外训练 selector，也不需要为估计 set utility 增加新的 forward pass。和 certainty-based sampler 相比，它不再把高置信但低内容量的 token 提前固定下来；和纯 info-gain sampler 相比，它显式引入了图像条件，避免语言侧高信息量 token 抢先主导生成轨迹。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个开源 dMLLM 和 7 个 benchmark，任务包括 captioning 与 VQA。论文最醒目的结果出现在高并行度设置：在 LaViDa 上每步解 8 个 token 时，VIG-Sampler 相比 Info-Gain Sampler 在 captioning benchmark 上平均提升 19.3 CIDEr，在 VQA benchmark 上平均提升 7.3 个 accuracy 点。COCO captioning 上还有一个更能说明效率-质量权衡的结果：VIG-Sampler 每步解 8 个 token，仍比 Info-Gain Sampler 每步只解 4 个 token 高 5.3 CIDEr，也就是在解码步数减半的情况下质量仍更好。论文的分析部分还强调两点：一是 certainty-based 规则确实倾向先解语法性、模板化 token；二是并行解码中 token redundancy 会显著拉低 set-level information gain，而 attention similarity 能较好刻画这种冗余。若全文里有分模型、分任务、分并行步长的表格，建议重点看这些切片，因为它们决定方法到底是“普适稳定”还是只在某个模型上成立。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点放在引言、方法和分析实验。先看 Figure 1 一类的动机示例，理解作者为什么认为“语言上有用”不等于“视觉上正确”。然后看方法部分里 token-level ordering 和 subset selection 两层设计，尤其是 attention score 与 redundancy penalty 的定义。实验部分优先看：1）不同 sampler 在不同并行 token 数下的对比；2）captioning 与 VQA 是否都稳定收益；3）有没有 ablation 区分“只用 image attention 排序”和“再加 redundancy penalty”的增益来源。预计 targets a specific weakness in diffusion multimodal LLM decoding: existing token selection rules for parallel denoising mostly rely on language-side confidence or information gain, but do not explicitly use the image when deciding which masked positions to reveal first. In dMLLMs, early decoded tokens immediately become conditioning context for later steps. If those tokens are linguistically informative but visually ungrounded, they can steer the whole generation trajectory toward a coherent yet image-inconsistent answer. The problem here is not training, but inference-time decoding policy: how to choose parallel tokens so that decoding remains informative while staying anchored to visual evidence, without extra training or extra forward passes.","importance":"This is not a central pretraining paper, since the main contribution is an inference strategy for multimodal diffusion LMs rather than a new objective or training recipe. Still, it matters for people working on non-AR language modeling. In diffusion or any-order generation, token order is no longer fixed by position, so the decoding policy itself becomes part of the effective model. The paper also makes a useful claim: token-to-image attention can serve as a practical proxy for both visual grounding and downstream usefulness. If that holds beyond this setting, it suggests that internal multimodal attention patterns can be reused for decoding control, instead of training a separate selector.","technical_approach":"$19","experiments":"The evaluation spans 3 open-source dMLLMs and 7 benchmarks covering captioning and VQA. The strongest result is under aggressive parallel decoding. On LaViDa with 8 tokens decoded per step, VIG-Sampler improves over the Info-Gain Sampler by an average of 19.3 CIDEr on captioning benchmarks and 7.3 accuracy points on VQA benchmarks. On COCO captioning, VIG-Sampler with 8 tokens per step still beats Info-Gain with only 4 tokens per step by 5.3 CIDEr, which means better quality despite using half as many decoding steps. The analysis also supports two claims: certainty-based decoding tends to reveal syntactic or template-like tokens too early, and set-level information gain is noticeably reduced by redundancy among parallel tokens, which attention similarity captures reasonably well. The most useful tables are likely the ones broken down by model, task, and number of tokens per step, since they show whether the method is broadly stable or only effective in a narrow regime.","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Second, the discrepancy between teacher and student distributions often leads to compounding e","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26872.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 OPD，又需要为每个目标单独准备专门 teacher，成本高，而且 student 始终受 teacher 上界和偏置限制。Self-OPD 想解决的就是这个夹缝问题：保留 step-wise supervision 的稳定性，同时去掉 teacher 依赖，并让单模型能在同一张图上同时满足多个奖励目标。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接相关性不在“语言模型预训练 The specific question is how to retain the main benefit of on-policy distillation—dense, per-step supervision on the model’s own trajectory—without relying on an external teacher. Pure RL is teacher-free but suffers from long-horizon credit assignment and high-variance updates, especially under mixed objectives. Teacher-based OPD is more stable, but every new objective needs a specialized teacher, and the student is bounded by teacher quality and bias. Self-OPD is designed to close that gap: teacher-free, step-wise, and compatible with multi-objective alignment.","importance":"This is not a pretraining recipe paper in the narrow LLM sense, but it is relevant to foundation-model training because it addresses a general issue: how to convert sparse terminal rewards into dense local learning signals for non-AR generative models. First, it reframes distillation-like supervision as self-exploration rather than teacher imitation. Second, it provides a practical local credit assignment mechanism for continuous denoising trajectories. Third, it handles multiple black-box rewards by fusing them at the reward level and using them only to rank local branches, instead of directly mixing conflicting gradients or teacher fields.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"$1c","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, not one that requires line-by-line derivation. parti","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26585.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CE","q-bio.QM","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文处理的是离散 的后验引导问题拆成了两个可审计的部分：proposal 贡献多少 reward，search/selection 贡献多少 reward，以及它们各自消耗多少 denoiser call。这个视角比单纯报告 reward 更有用，因为离散 diffusion LM 如果要成为 AR 之外的可用生成范式，推理期可控性和采样成本会是核心瓶颈。论文也提醒：reward 提升可能来自更强的选择压力，而不是更好的生成分布；这种提升会带来 mode collapse，常规 reward 指标不一定能反映。","technical_approach":"方法叫 GRAS，由两类改动组成。第一类改进 guided proposal。已有 GILC 类方法用 straight-through reward gradient 修正 clean-token logits，但梯度估计常来自单个 noisy sample，方差较大。论文针对可微 reward 使用 Gumbel-Rao / Rao-Blackwellized reveal，把一部分随机 reveal 的期望解析化，从而降低估计方差；针对不可微 reward 使用 leave-one-out baseline，减少粒子间 reward 波动带来的梯度噪声。这些改动不增加 denoiser 调用。第二类改进 search selection。传统 SMC/TDS 类方法常用固定 temperature resampling，但不同 denoising step 上 reward/value 的离散程度不同，固定温度会导致选择压力不稳定。论文提出 adaptive-temperature resampling，称为 AMC，把每一步的 value 标准化成 group-relative advantage，再用统一尺度控制选择强度。这样做的作用是让 resampling 对当前 step 的 reward spread 自适应，而不是把所有 step 当成同一尺度的 value 问题。与单独 gradient guidance 或单独 search 相比，GRAS 的重点不只是叠加二者，而是把 proposal 方差、selection temperature 和 denoiser-call budget 放在同一分析框架中。","experiments":"实验覆盖 regulatory DNA 和 protein design 等离散序列设计任务。论文强调 guided proposal 通常以较低成本拿到主要 reward 增益，随后收益趋于饱和；剩余提升主要来自 adaptive resampling，但代价是多样性下降。文中还把方法按 With a fixed data budget but relatively abundant compute, increasing parameter count helps only up to an optimal scale; beyond that point, models overfit and generalization worsens. We study this behavior across 10M-100M word pre-training budgets, two corpora, and multiple downstream evaluations, and find that optimal size depends strongly on both the data budget and the downstream target. We a","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26973.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个和主流 scaling law 不同的区间：数据预算固定且偏小，但算力并不紧张时，语言模型该怎么扩展。作者指出，在 10M 到 100M words 这类预算下，继续增大参数量并不会像 web-scale 预训练那样稳定带来收益，反而会更快过拟合；而标准 Transformer 在小模型区间还有一个结构性问题：词嵌入和 LM head 会吞掉很大一部分参数预算，导致“表示容量”和“每个 token 上能做多少计算”被绑在一起，难以单独调节。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它提醒人们不要把 Chinchilla 式数据-参数共同扩展的直觉直接搬到低数据 regime。高质量文本供给受限、持续预训练只拿到窄域语料、或 developmentally plausible 训练预算，这些场景都更接近文中设定。第二，它给出了一种架构层面的思路：在数据受限时，参数量更像正则项，而不是唯一的能力来源；如果能用权重共享把计算深度和参数量拆开，就可能在不明显增加过拟合风险的前提下继续榨取固定语料中的信号。这个结论对小数据预训练、continual pretrain、以及 tokenizer/embedding 占比过高的小模型设计都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"作者的核心主张是，标准 Transformer 在小规模预训练下“缩小得不自然”。hidden size 一降，attention 和 MLP 参数 roughly 按平方缩小，但 embedding 和输出词表矩阵只按线性缩小，于是模型越来越像一个大词表映射器，而不是一个有足够 token-wise computation 的序列模型。为了解耦这两件事，作者提出 RecursiveGPT，主要有两部分。\n\n第一部分是 factorized embeddings，用更低秩或更小中间维度的方式表示输入输出词表，减少词表相关参数占比，把预算腾给真正的序列计算模块。第二部分是 recursive Transformer，也就是把一个共享的 Transformer block 反复应用多次，用 recurrent depth 替代一部分独立层数。这样增加每 token 的计算量时，参数量增长很小。作者强调这不是为了推理省算，而是为了在固定数据下引入新的 compute scaling 轴。\n\n从全文表述看，RecursiveGPT 更接近 ALBERT 式跨层共享与 recurrent refinement 的结合，而不是引入外部记忆或显式状态空间。关键设计点在于：共享块重复多少次、是否在不同递归步使用不同的位置或步数编码、layer norm 和残差在共享场景下如何稳定训练、以及 factorized embedding 的维度如何选。论文把这些设计放在 BabyLM 风格的小数据预训练里系统比较，而不是只给一个单点模型。\n\n和已有路线相比，这篇工作有意避开两类常见做法。它没有主要依赖更激进的训练 recipe 去压过拟合，比如超大 weight decay、复杂 early stopping 或后期集成；也没有转向 masked LM、diffusion-style MLM 这类训练目标创新。作者要证明的是，即便训练目标不变，单靠架构把参数容量与计算深度拆开，也能在固定数据预算下得到更好的折中。","experiments":"实验覆盖 10M 到 100M words 两档主要数据预算，模型规模从约 13M 到 1.2B 参数，数据集用了两个语料源，并在多个下游和语言能力评测上比较。最重要的结果不是某个单点分数，而是几条趋势。\n\n第一，存在明显的最优参数规模，而且这个最优点随数据预算和评测目标变化。也就是说，在 10M words 下表现最好的模型规模，不会和 100M words 下相同；对 BLiMP、COMPS、以及其他 BabyLM 相关评测，最优规模也不一致。这直接反驳了“小数据时模型越大越好，只要正则够强”的简单叙述。\n\n第二，标准 Transformer 在小规模区间的参数利用率确实差。作者展示了 embedding/LM head 在总参数中的占比随模型缩小而快速上升，同时性能并没有随参数增加单调提升，反而在超过某个规模后出现泛化下降。RecursiveGPT 在相近甚至更低参数量下，通过更大的 recurrent depth 获得更好的结果，说明额外计算而非额外独立参数更适合这个 regime。\n\n第三，RecursiveGPT 在 10M 和 100M words 两个预算下都优于对应的标准 Transformer 基线，并且和 BabyLM 2025 的强系统保持竞争力。摘要没有给出完整数字，但正文强调其结果在 BLiMP、COMPS 以及补充评测如 BLiMP Supplement、EWoK、Entity Tracking 上都处于有竞争力的区间。更重要的是，作者做了消融，分别考察 factorized embeddings 和 recursive depth 的作用，结论是两者都有贡献，但递归共享带来的“增加计算不显著增参”是主要收益来源。\n\n第四，论文还分析了过拟合行为与训练曲线。随着参数量增大，训练损失继续下降，但验证和下游指标先升后降；而递归模型在相似训练预算下能把更多计算放在 token transformation 上，因此在固定数据上泛化更稳。这部分对做 limited-data scaling 的人比最终榜单更有参考价值。","reading_guide":"泛读建议很明确。先看 Introduction 和 Background，把作者讨论的 regime 立住：这里不是 web-scale scaling，而是 data-limited, compute-abundant。然后直接看模型设计部分，重点关注两件事：为什么 embedding 在小模型里会成为结构性瓶颈，以及 fixed and relatively small data budget, but with enough compute to keep training longer or apply more computation per token. In this setting, increasing parameter count is not reliably helpful. Beyond an optimum, larger models overfit and generalization worsens. from richer target distributions—powered, product, or tilted versions of the base distribution—without training a new model or relying on an external reward model. The main difficulty is that these targets are typically unnormalized and hard to sample from: importance sampling can suffer from poor coverage, while vanilla MCMC can mix slowly on multimodal sequence distributions.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is conceptual and diagnostic. This gives a cleaner way to reason about base-model coverage: if the pretrained model already assigns non-negligible mass to good solutions, better inference can concentrate that mass without changing parameters. It also helps separate two failure modes that matter in pretraining analysis: the model may not have learned the solution, or it may have learned it but placed too little probability mass on the useful region for naive sampling to find it.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"The experiments use mathematical reasoning to show that generation quality can be improved by changing only the sampling process. Unlike autoregressive systems, whose key-value (KV) cache can be reused for shared prefixes, DLLMs couple the KV states of shared context tokens with evolving generated tokens through bidirectional attention, making naive cache reuse stale while full recomputation is expensive. We present ACache, an affix-oriented cache reuse mechanism for sh","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26140.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 diffusion LLM 推理里的一个具体瓶颈：如何像 AR 模型那样复用缓存，但又不被双向注意力带来的 stale KV 问题拖垮。AR 模型的 prefix cache 之所以好用，是因为历史 token 的表示不会被未来生成 token 反向影响；而 DLLM 在每一步去噪时做全局双向注意力，连共享 prompt 或中间固定片段的 KV 都会随着待生成 token 的变化而变化。直接复用会掉质量，完全重算又太贵。ACache 的问题定义很清楚：在保证生成质量的前提下，只重算真正关键的 affix tokens，其余共享 span 的 cache 尽量复用。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接影响有限，对新型语言建模范式的落地影响更大。Diffusion LM 近两年开始从小模型和概念验证走向 billion-scale，但推理效率一直是它和 AR 竞争时最现实的短板之一。很多人讨论 DLLM 时只看并行解码潜力，忽略了每步全序列重算带来的系统成本。ACache 的价值在于把这个问题拆细：不是“能不能缓存”，而是“哪些 token 的缓存必须更新”。如果这个思路成立，说明 DLLM 的系统优化空间并不只在 schedule、步数压缩或 speculative denoising，也在更细粒度的上下文状态管理。对做建模的人，这会反过来影响训练时是否要鼓励更局部、可缓存的依赖结构。","technical_approach":"核心做法是 affix-oriented cache reuse。作者不把共享上下文限制为 prefix，而是推广到任意共享连续片段 affix，可以在开头、中间或结尾。这个设定利用了 DLLM 的双向注意力特性：既然模型天然能看两边，上下文复用也不必像 AR 那样只围绕左前缀设计。\n\n难点在于，哪些 affix token 的 KV 会因为当前 masked generation tokens 的变化而显著失效。ACache 的答案是引入 Anchor Tokens。作者通过测量 masked generation tokens 到 affix tokens 的 cross-attention importance，找出对当前生成最敏感的一小部分 affix token，只对这些 token 重算 KV；其余 affix token 继续复用旧 cache。这样做等于把“整段共享上下文是否需要刷新”变成“少量关键位置是否需要刷新”。\n\n从系统实现看，ACache 建在 Fast-dLLM 之上，用 selective Anchor recomputation 替代 periodic full-cache recomputation。也就是说，它不是完全推翻现有 DLLM cache 框架，而是在已有近似缓存策略上进一步细化重算粒度。作者还在 Nano-vLLM 上做了 shared-prefix 原型，验证在更接近真实 serving 的 paged memory management 和 continuous batching 场景下，选择性重算仍然有收益。\n\n和已有方法相比，差异点比较明确。dLLM-Cache 更偏向不对称更新 prompt / response，并用特征相似度决定部分 response token 是否重算；dKV-Cache 更关注 decoded tokens 的块级延迟重算；Fast-dLLM 通过更高效的推理框架和周期性刷新降低成本。ACache 的新意在于把“共享上下文”本身作为优化对象，并且把重算单位从整段 cache 缩到 request-specific 的 token 子集。","experiments":"从正文可见的关键结果有三层。第一，质量恢复：相对直接复用 affix cache 带来的明显精度下降，ACache 在多个 benchmark 上能把质量拉回，同时只需重算大约 20% 的 affix tokens。这个比例很关键，说明重要性分布是稀疏的，至少在作者测试的设置里，不需要大面积刷新共享上下文。第二，系统收益：在 Nano-vLLM 的 shared-prefix 原型中，ACache 将 recompute latency 最多降低 55.7%，端到端吞吐最高提升 1.68×。这说明收益不只存在于理论 FLOPs，而能穿透到带分页内存和连续批处理的实际推理栈。第三，适用范围：作者强调它适用于 shared spans beyond prefixes，这一点比单纯 prefix cache 更贴近实际多请求服务，因为模板、检索片段、工具返回文本、对话历史摘要都可能以中间片段形式复用。\n\n从研究者角度，更值得注意的是消融逻辑而不是单个数字：Anchor Token 选择策略是否稳定、重算比例和质量之间的曲线是否平滑、不同 affix 位置是否影响收益、以及在不同 denoising steps 上 stale cache 的伤害是否累积。如果正文里有这些图，建议重点看，因为它们决定这个方法是“工程技巧”还是“可推广机制”。","reading_guide":"这篇虽然标成 skim，但如果你在跟 diffusion LM，可以比普通 skim 多花一点时间。先看 Background，把作者放在 D3PM、SEDD、MDLM、LLaDA、Dream 这条线上的定位弄清楚。然后直接看 Figure 1 和方法部分，理解为什么 DLLM 的 cache stale 问题和 AR 完全不同。接着看 Anchor Token 的定义与选择方式，这是整篇最核心的机制。实验部分优先看三类图表：质量-重算比例曲线、不同 affix 位置的结果、以及 Nano-vLLM 原型下的 latency / throughput 数据。预计 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主要内容。若要深挖，建议思考两个问题：一是 Anchor 重要性是否能在训练时被显式建模；二是这种稀疏重算是否能和更少 denoising steps、speculative decoding 或 hybrid AR+diffusion 框架结合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a concrete inference bottleneck in diffusion LLMs: how to reuse cache without suffering from stale KV states under bidirectional attention. In autoregressive models, prefix caching works because past token states do not depend on future generated tokens. In DLLMs, each denoising step uses full bidirectional attention, so even the KV states of shared prompt or context tokens become coupled to the evolving masked response. Naive reuse hurts quality, while full recomputation is expensive. ACache asks a narrower and more useful question: can we recompute only the truly critical shared tokens and reuse the rest?","importance":"Its direct impact on pretraining is limited, but its importance for alternative language-modeling paradigms is real. Diffusion LMs are becoming more credible at scale, yet inference efficiency remains one of their main practical weaknesses relative to AR models. Much of the discussion around DLLMs focuses on parallel decoding, but that misses the cost of repeated full-sequence recomputation. ACache is useful because it reframes the systems problem from “can DLLMs cache at all?” to “which shared tokens actually need refreshing?” If that framing holds, it suggests that DLLM efficiency is not only about reducing denoising steps or improving schedules, but also about finer-grained state management. That can eventually feed back into model design and training objectives.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"Although marked as skim, this deserves a bit more attention if you follow diffusion LMs. Start with the background to place the work in the D3PM/SEDD/MDLM/LLaDA/Dream line. Existing KV cache eviction methods score tokens using the model's attention distribution or, in attention-free variants, each key's distance from a global reference point. Using a controlled leave-one-out probe, we find that attention magnitude is unrelated to a token's causal contribution to the answer (Spearman $\\rho=-0.004$), challenging the pre","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_27128.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长上下文推理里的一个老问题：KV cache 太大，必须做 eviction，但现有方法大多默认“被高注意力看到的 token 更重要”，或者用“离全局 key 均值更远”来近似重要性。论文先用 leave-one-out 因果探针直接检验这个前提，发现 attention magnitude 与 token 对最终答案的真实边际贡献几乎不相关，在其长文问答设定上 Spearman ρ = -0.004，p = 0.96。也就是说，很多 eviction 方法依赖的打分信号并不对应真正的因果必要性。作者因此把问题改写为：在固定预算下，怎样识别“删掉后还能被上下文中其他位置恢复”的冗余 token，并用这个结构性信号修补已有 eviction 决策。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在于提出了一个全新的压缩器，而在于它给 KV eviction 研究补了一块很关键的诊断：attention 不是可靠的因果重要性代理。这个结论对推理优化和训练后分析都有价值。前者体现在，很多 cache 压缩方法可能是在利用 attention 的经验相关性，而不是抓住真正的信息保留条件；后者体现在，它提示我们在分析 transformer 内部记忆时，要区分“被访问”与“不可替代”这两件事。TwinKV 本身是 training-free、attention-free、可组合的 repair pass，不要求替换现有系统，只需在已有策略选完保留集合后做一次交换修补。这种设计很工程化，适合直接插到现有 serving 栈里。论文的贡献量级偏中等：不是范式变化，但对小模型、边缘设备、严格显存预算场景有现实价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心很简单，但抓得比较准。作者把 token 的重要性定义成“唯一性”的反面：如果某个 token 的 key 在上下文其他非相邻位置存在近重复，那么删掉它后，模型仍可能通过保留下来的近重复项恢复这部分信息；如果没有重复，它更可能是不可替代的。具体做法是：对每个 token 的 key，统计有多少其他非相邻 token 与它的 key cosine similarity 超过阈值，这个 twin count 越高，说明冗余越强，重要性越低。这里有两个设计点值得注意。第一，使用 key 而不是 value 或 hidden state，是因为 eviction 决策发生在 attention 检索层面，key 更直接反映“可被匹配到的记忆地址”；第二，排除相邻 token，是为了避免把局部连续文本的平凡相似性误判成真正的语义冗余。\n\n更有意思的是它不是 standalone policy，而是 repair pass。给定任意已有 eviction 方法输出的 retained set 和 evicted set，TwinKV 检查两类错误：一类是某个被删 token 在 retained set 中没有近重复，属于 unrecoverable eviction；另一类是某个被留 token 在 retained set 内有冗余副本，属于 wasted retention。然后在不改变总预算的前提下做 swap：把前者换回，把后者换出。这样做的好处是，它不需要知道被包装方法内部如何打分，也不需要重新排序全体 token，只利用最终保留结果做局部修正。论文还专门把这种 pairwise redundancy 与 anchor-based 方法区分开：后者用单一全局参考点衡量“离群程度”，更像异常检测；TwinKV 判断的是“是否有替身”，是局部关系而不是全局几何位置，因此两者的归纳偏置不同。","experiments":"实验围绕两个问题展开。第一，attention 是否真能反映因果贡献。作者在真实长上下文 QA 上做 chunk-level leave-one-out：移除一个上下文块，看正确答案 log-prob 降多少，把这个边际损失与原始前向中的 attention 质量做相关性比较。结果几乎零相关，直接动摇了 attention-based eviction 的核心假设。\n\n第二，TwinKV 作为 repair pass 是否能稳定改进现有方法。作者在 Qwen3-4B 上，把 TwinKV 叠加到四种近期 eviction 策略上：StreamingLLM、PyramidKV、SnapKV、ExpectedAttention；评测覆盖 LongBench、LooGLE、RULER，以及一个短上下文控制集 MMLU-Pro；压缩率测试 {0.3, 0.5, 0.7}。结果不是“全面提升”，而是很有条件性：对 StreamingLLM，最多 64% 的评测单元得到改善；对 PyramidKV 是 57%；对 SnapKV 是 41%；对 ExpectedAttention 只有 16%。作者把 ExpectedAttention 改进有限解释为其本身已接近若干 benchmark 的上限，repair pass 更容易打扰好决策而不是修正坏决策。另一个稳定现象是，TwinKV 在更紧的预算下更有帮助，在最宽松压缩率时平均增益最小。这和论文的因果叙事一致：预算越紧，每一次误删越致命，结构性“唯一性”信号的价值越高。\n\n从全文表述看，这篇论文更强调 win-rate 和配置覆盖面，而不是单个 benchmark 上的大幅绝对分数提升。它的卖点是低侵入、零训练、对多种基线可组合，而不是在某一条曲线上把最优点抬得很高。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1。关键不是 TwinKV 的公式，而是作者先做了一个很直接的因果检验，把 attention-as-importance 这个常见前提拆掉。这个诊断比方法本身更值得记住。\n\n然后看 Section 2 的 leave-one-out probe 设计，确认它的因果贡献定义、chunk 粒度、相关性统计方式。这里决定了论文论证是否站得住。\n\nSection 3 看 repair pass 机制，重点理解为什么它做 swap 而不是重新打分全局排序，以及 pairwise redundancy 与 centroid distance 的区别。若只想掌握方法，读到这里基本够了。\n\n实验部分建议重点看：1）不同 baseline 上的 win-rate 分布；2）不同压缩率下的变化；3）短上下文控制集 MMLU-Pro 的结果，用来判断 TwinKV 是否只是在长上下文里修补记忆冗余，而不是普遍提升模型能力。预计 20–30 分钟可以抓住主要内容。若要深挖，可以继续看阈值选择、非相邻约束、不同层或不同头的敏感性分析；这些细节决定它能否迁移到别的模型和 serving 实现。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies KV-cache eviction under long-context inference, where memory grows linearly with sequence length and quickly becomes the dominant bottleneck, especially for small models under tight deployment budgets. The key claim is diagnostic before it is algorithmic: most prior eviction methods assume that attention mass is a useful proxy for token importance, or use distance from a global key centroid as a surrogate. The authors test this premise with a leave-one-out causal probe and find that attention received by a context chunk is essentially uncorrelated with its true marginal contribution to the correct answer on long-context QA (Spearman rho = -0.004, p = 0.96). The problem is then reframed as identifying tokens whose information would survive eviction because a near-equivalent copy remains elsewhere in context.","importance":"Its main value is not that it introduces a new end-to-end compressor, but that it challenges a widely used scoring assumption in KV eviction: being attended to is not the same as being causally necessary. That matters for inference research, because many cache-compression heuristics may be exploiting weak empirical regularities rather than preserving irreplaceable information. It also matters for mechanistic analysis of transformer memory, since the paper separates access frequency from recoverability. TwinKV itself is lightweight: training-free, attention-free, and composable as a repair pass on top of existing policies. This makes it practical for serving systems. The contribution is moderate in scope but concrete, with the clearest relevance in memory-constrained long-context deployment rather than in core pretraining.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"$24","reading_guide":"$25","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 leave-one-out probe 和 TwinKV swap 规则；attention 分数近似无因果信号这个数值得在内部长上下文压缩里复验。","en":"Read the leave-one-out probe and the TwinKV swap rule; the near-zero causal signal in attention scores is worth reproducing for internal long-context compression."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Hong Chen","Yudong Zeng","Yongwei Huang","Zuhao Ouyang","Junyan Zhang","Xuming Hu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["KV-cache","long-context","inference-efficiency","cache-eviction","attention-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27128","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.27128","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.27206","title":"PACE: A Unified Condense-and-Extract Paradigm for Fast VLM Inference","abstract":"Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate exceptional visual reasoning capabilities, yet their inference costs escalate rapidly with the proliferation of visual tokens. fine-gra","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_27206.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在不改模型、不训练的前提下，加速 两阶段组成，核心点是把压缩前移到 ViT 之前，同时在 ViT 之后用更稳健的信号选 token。\n1) Condense（APC, Adaptive Pixel Compressor）：\n- 先用轻量“feature preview”估计图像的信息密度/细节复杂度。\n- 在像素域对输入做自适应压缩（可以理解为在预算约束下重新分配分辨率/采样密度），让 ViT 看到的是更小的像素量，但尽量保留全局布局线索。\n- 这样直接减少 ViT 编码 FLOPs 与延迟，而不是只砍 LLM 上下文。\n2) Extract（DDAE, Dynamic Dual-Attention Extractor）：\n- 融合两种注意力信号：ViT 自注意力（反映视觉内部显著性/结构）与 LLM 跨模态注意力（反映对当前语言任务的相关性）。\n- ChartQA、DocVQA）在严格预算下掉点明显（Figure 3）。\n当前提供的正文截断未包含更细的表格数据（不同 token budget 下的曲线、与 DivPrune/VisionZip 等基线的逐项对比、以及 APC vs DDAE 的消融）。复核时建议重点看：\n- 仅 APC（只省 ViT）与仅 DDAE（只省 LLM）各自贡献；\n- 在文本密集/细节敏感 benchmark 这类压缩更鲁棒。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Speeding up VLM inference under massive visual token counts by addressing both bottlenecks—expensive high-res vision encoding and LLM-side prefill/attention—while preserving global layout and fine details under tight token budgets, without any training.","importance":"This is an inference/efficiency paper rather than a pretraining-method paper, but it matters operationally for multimodal foundation models. It makes a concrete point that post-encoder token pruning only tackles the LLM half; the vision encoder can dominate latency at high resolution. That observation feeds back into pretraining choices around resolution, patch size, and visual tokenization. The two-stage design (pixel-level condensation before ViT + token extraction after ViT) is also a reusable template for building controllable degradation and could inspire robustness-oriented curricula during pretraining.","technical_approach":"PACE is a training-free, plug-and-play Condense-and-Extract pipeline:\n1) Condense via APC (Adaptive Pixel Compressor): run a lightweight feature preview to estimate information density, then adaptively condense the image in pixel space before the ViT forward pass. This reduces the pixel budget and directly cuts vision-encoder compute while trying to preserve holistic layout cues.\n2) Extract via DDAE (Dynamic Dual-Attention Extractor): select post-encoder visual tokens using a confidence-weighted fusion of (a) ViT self-attention signals (visual saliency/structure) and (b) LLM cross-modal attention signals (task relevance). The dual-source attention is meant to retain both anchors (layout) and critical details (e.g., small text, chart elements) under strict budgets.\n3) Deployment: no finetuning; designed to be inserted into existing VLM pipelines.","experiments":"Key quantitative claim available from the provided text: on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, PACE discards 90% of visual tokens while preserving >93% of original performance and achieves ~3.1× TTFT speedup.\nThe excerpt also highlights diagnostic evidence: latency profiling shows a dual bottleneck (ViT encoding + LLM prefill), and prior pruning methods degrade sharply on detail-sensitive tasks like ChartQA/DocVQA under low budgets.\nThe provided excerpt does not include full tables/ablations. Recent hardware support for low-precision formats, ranging from MXFP8 to ultra-low-bit formats such as MXFP4 and HiF4, has accelerated research into efficient MLLM training and deployment. In this work, we present a systematic study of these quantization schemes in representative MLLMs that span both video generation and reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_26581.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是多模态大模型在超低比特量化，尤其是 FP4 训练/部署时，为什么会明显失稳，以及怎样在不回退到高精度、不改模型结构的前提下，把主要误差源压住。论文的核心判断很明确：在代表性的 MLLM 里，4-bit 退化的主因不是权重量化，而是激活量化；更具体地说，是某些模块里重尾分布和离群值导致的激活失真。作者要解决的是这个训练期动态出现、且模块依赖很强的 activation outlier 问题。","importance":"对基座研究的价值不在于“又一个量化技巧”，而在于它把多模态统一模型里的数值脆弱性拆得比较清楚。第一，论文给出一个对 MLLM 很实用的结论：视觉侧通常比语言侧更怕超低比特，且不同架构的脆弱模块并不相同，因此不能直接套文本 LLM 的统一量化 recipe。第二，它把关注点从权重转向激活，这对训练系统、低精度预训练、以及未来 native multimodal pretrain 的硬件友好设计都有启发。第三，提出的 RFQ 属于一种训练感知的误差补偿思路：不是试图完全消灭 outlier，而是显式编码量化残差。这种思路对更广义的低精度训练也有参考价值。","technical_approach":"论文先做系统性诊断，再给出方法。诊断部分覆盖两类代表性 MLLM：Wan2.2 这类多模态生成模型，以及 Qwen3-VL 这类多模态推理模型；量化格式包括 MXFP8、MXFP4、HiF4，并且不是只量化某一层，而是覆盖视觉编码器、文本编码器、注意力、MLP、多模态投影层、MoE 等关键组件。作者通过模块级 ablation 发现，FP8 基本接近无损，但 FP4 的退化高度异质：Wan2.2 中视觉生成主干更脆弱，文本编码器相对稳；Qwen3-VL 中视觉编码器最敏感，但语言主干也会出现明显退化。进一步拆分 weight-only 与 activation quantization 后，结论是激活量化贡献了主要性能损失。\n\n在此基础上，作者提出 Residual Fallback Quantization, RFQ。它不是常见的 threshold-based outlier fallback：后者通常会检测大值并把少量 outlier 保留在高精度或单独路径里，这在训练和硬件实现上都比较麻烦，也容易引入离散选择。RFQ 的做法更连续：先把激活做主路径量化，得到一个 FP4 表示；再显式计算量化残差，并把这个残差通过第二条低比特路径再量化一次，用于重构。也就是说，输出近似是 q(x) + q_res(x - q(x))，而不是“普通值走低比特，离群值走高比特”。这样做有几个好处：\n\n1. 全流程可微，适合端到端训练；\n2. 不需要阈值选择，避免不同模块、不同训练阶段阈值难调；\n3. 不依赖 full-precision fallback，保持 FP4 友好；\n4. 更适合处理训练中动态变化的 outlier，因为它补偿的是当前量化误差，而不是依赖静态校准统计。\n\n从方法定位看，RFQ 介于 PTQ 的静态平滑和 QAT 的显式混精度回退之间。它承认 FP4 无法完整容纳激活动态范围，因此直接对误差建模，而不是强行把原分布压进单一路径量化器里。","experiments":"从已给出的全文内容可以明确读到几类关键结果。\n\n1. 精度格式对性能影响分层明显：MXFP8 基本接近无损，而 MXFP4/HiF4 会带来显著退化。这说明 8-bit 在当前 MLLM 上已经比较成熟，真正困难的是 4-bit 端到端训练。\n2. 退化来源上，activation quantization 明显重于 weight quantization。作者做了大量消融来区分两者贡献，结论是只压权重通常还能接受，一旦把激活也压到 FP4，性能会明显下滑。\n3. 模块敏感性高度异质。Wan2.2 的 WanDiT 视觉生成主干是主要瓶颈，UMT5 文本编码器相对稳；Qwen3-VL 的视觉编码器最敏感，但语言模块也不是完全安全区。这说明“统一全模型同一量化策略”在 MLLM 上往往不够好。\n4. RFQ 能在不使用高精度回退的情况下恢复一部分 FP4 损失。摘要里没有给出具体百分点，但从叙述看，作者强调 RFQ 主要改善的是由激活 outlier 引起的训练不稳定和精度塌陷，而不是对所有模块平均小修小补。\n\n如果正文表格提供了任务指标，建议重点看两类数字：一是 FP8、FP4、HiF4 在生成与推理任务上的相对差距；二是 weight-only、act-only、W+A 量化的拆分结果。对预训练研究者来说，这比最终单一 benchmark 分数更有信息量，因为它直接告诉你误差预算该花在哪一侧。","reading_guide":"这篇适合按“诊断优先，方法其次”的顺序读。先看实验设计和模块级 ablation，再看 RFQ 细节。具体建议如下。\n\n第一，先读引言和实验总表，确认作者研究的不是单一模型或单一任务，而是生成型与推理型两类 MLLM。这样后面看到模块差异时更容易判断哪些结论可迁移。\n\n第二，重点看 activation vs weight 的拆分实验，以及各模块的敏感性排序。这里是全文最有信息密度的部分，因为它回答了“为什么 FP4 不行”而不是只展示“FP4 结果差”。\n\n第三，再看 RFQ 的公式和实现细节，尤其是残差如何量化、是否引入额外存储/带宽、训练时和推理时的开销如何。若你关心低精度预训练，这部分要结合系统代价一起读，而不是只看精度恢复。\n\n第四，若正文有激活分布图、离群值统计、不同层的误差热图，建议细看。这些图通常比最终 benchmark 更能说明问题。\n\n作为 skim 论文，泛读 30 到 45 分钟可以抓住主线；若你正在做 FP8/FP4 训练、视觉 tokenizer、或 unified multimodal pretrain 的系统优化，建议花 1 到 1.5 小时把消融和附录实现细节读完。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why multimodal LLMs become fragile under ultra-low-bit quantization, especially FP4, and how to recover performance without reverting to higher precision or changing the model architecture. Its central diagnosis is clear: in representative MLLMs, the main source of FP4 degradation is not weight quantization but activation quantization, driven by heavy-tailed activation distributions and outliers in specific modules.","importance":"The value of the paper is less about adding another quantization trick and more about clarifying where numerical fragility lives in multimodal foundation models. First, it shows that visual components are often more sensitive than language components under ultra-low precision, and that the vulnerable modules differ across architectures. That is an important warning against reusing text-only LLM quantization recipes for MLLMs. Second, it shifts attention from weights to activations, which matters for low-precision training, hardware-aware pretraining, and future native multimodal model design. Third, the proposed RFQ method embodies a useful idea for low-bit training more broadly: instead of trying to eliminate outliers entirely, explicitly model and quantize the residual error introduced by the main quantizer.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"$28","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看量化误差分解和 RFQ 消融即可；它强化了一个工程 prior：MLLM 的 4-bit 难点首先在 activation，不在权重。","en":"Skim the error decomposition and RFQ ablations; the useful prior is that 4-bit MLLM pain is primarily activation-side, not weight-side."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Tanzila Rahman","Mehran Taghian Jazi","Yunke Peng","Zhuang Ma","Anandharaju Durai Raju","Yao Wang","Xing Huang","Hei Yi Mak","Shadan Golestan","Hoang Le","Yonghan Dong","Wei Guo","Yaoyuan Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["quantization","MLLM","activation-outliers","serving","low-precision"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26581","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.26581","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.26926","title":"A Layer Importance Metric for Quantization Accounting for the Speed-Quality Trade-off in Autoregressive Models","abstract":"Small language models (sLLMs) are nowadays hosted on devices with limited memory and computational budget. metric，由质量分数和速度分数组成。质量分数用 SQNR，即 signal-to-quantization-noise ratio，衡量经过 SA-PTQ 算子量化后该模块的信息保留程度，并做归一化以便不同层比较。速度分数来自 roofline latency model，用硬件带宽、算力和算子访存/计算特征估计量化某个层可能带来的吞吐收益，不需要每种配置都实际部署测试。最后用一个权重参数 alpha 把两者合成单一指标，允许使用者根据任务偏好在质量保留和速度收益之间移动。论文先对 Gemma 3 1B 做架构 profiling，指出 FFN 和 embedding matrix 是优先分析的量化目标，因为它们对推理延迟和权重搬运有较大贡献。","experiments":"实验围绕 Gemma 3 1B 的架构 profiling、FFN 与 embedding 模块的 SQNR
